56 // Make compile time checks if blas::gemm can handle the given input matrix. If it can, simply forward the matrix.
57 // Otherwise, return a copy with the given value type T, layout policy LP and container policy CP.
58 template <typename T, typename LP, typename CP, MemoryMatrix C, Matrix A>
59 248 decltype(auto) get_gemm_matrix(A &&a) {
60 if constexpr (requires { blas::get_array(a); } and std::is_same_v<get_value_t<A>, T>) {
61 if constexpr (MemoryMatrix<A>
62 or (blas::is_conj_array_expr<A>
63 and ((blas::has_F_layout<C> and blas::has_C_layout<A>) or (blas::has_C_layout<C> and blas::has_F_layout<A>)))) {
64 232 return std::forward<A>(a);
65 } else {
66 11 return matrix<T, LP, CP>{a};
67 }
68 } else {
69 5 return matrix<T, LP, CP>{a};
70 }
71 }

91 // Get the layout policy for a given array type.

97 /**
98 * @brief Compute the matrix-matrix product of two nda::matrix objects.
99 *
100 * @details This function computes the matrix-matrix product \f$ \mathrm{op}_1(\mathbf{A}) \mathrm{op}_2(\mathbf{B})
101 * \f$, where \f$ \mathbf{A} \f$ and \f$ \mathbf{B} \f$ are \f$ m \times k \f$ and \f$ k \times n \f$ matrices
102 * respectively, \f$ \mathrm{op}_i \f$ can be some lazy operation, e.g. nda::conj, nda::sin, etc.
103 *
104 * We try to call nda::blas::gemm whenever possible, i.e. when the value type of the result is compatible with
105 * nda::is_blas_lapack_v, even if this requires to make copies of the input arrays/views. Otherwise, we perform a very
106 * naive and inefficient matrix-matrix multiplication manually.
107 *
108 * Therefore, if performance is important, users should make sure to pass input arrays/views which are compatible with
109 * nda::blas::gemm.
110 *
111 * @note The layout of the returned matrix depends on the layout of the input matrices. If both input matrices are in
112 * Fortran-layout, the returned matrix is also in Fortran layout. Otherwise, it is in C-layout.
113 *
114 * @warning This function might make copies of the input arrays/views. When working on the device memory space, this
115 * may lead to runtime errors if the copying fails.
116 *
117 * @tparam A nda::Matrix type.
118 * @tparam B nda::Matrix type.
119 * @param a Input matrix \f$ \mathbf{A} \f$ of size \f$ m \times k \f$.
120 * @param b Input matrix \f$ \mathbf{B} \f$ of size \f$ k \times n \f$.
121 * @return Resulting matrix of the matrix-matrix multiplication of size \f$ m \times n \f$.
122 */
123 template <Matrix A, Matrix B>
124 157 auto matmul(A &&a, B &&b) { // NOLINT (temporary views are allowed here)
125 // get the return type
126 using value_t = decltype(a(0, 0) * b(0, 0));
127 using layout_pol = std::conditional_t<get_layout_info<A>.stride_order == get_layout_info<B>.stride_order, detail::get_layout_policy<A>, C_layout>;
128 using cont_pol = heap<mem::common_addr_space<A, B>>;
129 using return_t = matrix<value_t, layout_pol, cont_pol>;
130
131 // result matrix (MSAN complains if it is not initialized)
132
